--- FILE: map.js ---
import fs from 'fs';
import path from 'path';

const outputFile = 'project-map.txt';
const projectRoot = './'; 

// Folders/Files to ignore
const ignore = ['node_modules', '.next', '.git', '.sanity', 'package-lock.json', '.gitignore'];
// Extensions to skip (binary/assets)
const skipExtensions = ['.ico', '.png', '.jpg', '.jpeg', '.svg', '.gif'];

function getFiles(dir, fileList = []) {
  const files = fs.readdirSync(dir);
  files.forEach(file => {
    if (ignore.includes(file)) return;
    const filePath = path.join(dir, file);

    if (fs.statSync(filePath).isDirectory()) {
      getFiles(filePath, fileList);
    } else {
      const ext = path.extname(file).toLowerCase();
      if (!skipExtensions.includes(ext)) {
        fileList.push(filePath);
      }
    }
  });
  return fileList;
}

const allFiles = getFiles(projectRoot);
let output = 'PROJECT CODE MAP (Assets Excluded)\n==================================\n\n';

allFiles.forEach(file => {
  // Prevent the script from reading its own output file if it already exists
  if (path.basename(file) === outputFile) return;

  output += `\n--- FILE: ${file} ---\n`;
  try {
    output += fs.readFileSync(file, 'utf8');
  } catch (err) {
    output += '[Could not read file]';
  }
  output += '\n\n';
});

fs.writeFileSync(outputFile, output);
console.log(`Success! Your clean code map is saved in ${outputFile}`);

--- FILE: README.md ---
# 🌋 The Stone World (React + Vite Edition)

> **"From zero to the stars through the power of science."**

A production-grade **Vite + React 18 + Tailwind CSS** game where civilization is rebuilt across 8 scientific eras.

---

## ⚡ Quickstart

```bash
# 1. Install dependencies (if not already installed)
npm install

# 2. Run local development server
npm run dev

# 3. Build for production (e.g. for Vercel, Netlify, GitHub Pages)
npm run build
```

The game will launch locally at `http://localhost:3000`.

---

## 🛠️ Tech Stack

* **Framework:** React 18
* **Build Tool:** Vite 6 (Ultra-fast HMR)
* **Styling:** Tailwind CSS 3
* **Icons:** Lucide-react
* **Celebrations:** Canvas-Confetti
* **Audio:** Native Web Audio API procedural synthesis (zero audio downloads)
* **AI Mentor:** Dual-mode (Instant offline Socratic deduction + optional live Google Gemini API)

---

## 📂 Project Structure

```
the-stone-world-react/
├── index.html
├── package.json
├── vite.config.js
├── tailwind.config.js
├── src/
│   ├── main.jsx                 # React root
│   ├── App.jsx                  # Main application state machine & tabs
│   ├── index.css                # Tailwind directives
│   ├── data/
│   │   └── gameData.js          # 8 Eras, bilingual elements & recipes
│   ├── hooks/
│   │   └── useSoundFX.js        # Web Audio synthesizer hook
│   ├── components/
│   │   ├── Header.jsx           # Stats, era badge, localization switch
│   │   ├── SettlementCanvas.jsx # Dynamic evolving settlement illustration
│   │   ├── Crucible.jsx         # Synthesis laboratory & celebration
│   │   ├── InventoryGrid.jsx    # Filterable unlocked discoveries
│   │   ├── ElementInspector.jsx # Deep lore & science principles
│   │   ├── MentorDrawer.jsx     # Socratic AI chat companion
│   │   └── GreatArchive.jsx     # 8 Eras civilization timeline
│   └── puzzles/
│       └── LeveragePuzzle.jsx   # Era 1 Archimedes' lever physics simulation
```



--- FILE: src\App.jsx ---
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { Header } from './components/Header';
import { SettlementCanvas } from './components/SettlementCanvas';
import { Crucible } from './components/Crucible';
import { InventoryGrid } from './components/InventoryGrid';
import { ElementInspector } from './components/ElementInspector';
import { MentorDrawer } from './components/MentorDrawer';
import { GreatArchive } from './components/GreatArchive';
import { LeveragePuzzle } from './puzzles/LeveragePuzzle';
import { ERAS, ELEMENTS } from './data/gameData';
import { useSoundFX } from './hooks/useSoundFX';
import { Anvil, Puzzle, BookOpen, KeyRound } from 'lucide-react';

const STORAGE_KEY = 'the_stone_world_react_save_v1';

export default function App() {
  const [lang, setLang] = useState('en');
  const [isMuted, setIsMuted] = useState(false);
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState('forge');
  const [currentEra, setCurrentEra] = useState(1);
  const [unlockedEras, setUnlockedEras] = useState([1]);
  const [inventory, setInventory] = useState(['stone', 'wood', 'dry_leaves']);
  const [stats, setStats] = useState({ food: 40, tech: 10, stability: 50 });
  const [solvedPuzzles, setSolvedPuzzles] = useState({});
  const [slot1, setSlot1] = useState(null);
  const [slot2, setSlot2] = useState(null);
  const [inspectId, setInspectId] = useState('stone');
  const [apiKey, setApiKey] = useState('');
  const [isSettingsOpen, setIsSettingsOpen] = useState(false);

  const soundFX = useSoundFX(isMuted);

  // Load from localStorage on mount
  useEffect(() => {
    try {
      const saved = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY);
      if (saved) {
        const parsed = JSON.parse(saved);
        if (parsed.currentEra) setCurrentEra(parsed.currentEra);
        if (parsed.unlockedEras) setUnlockedEras(parsed.unlockedEras);
        if (parsed.inventory) setInventory(parsed.inventory);
        if (parsed.stats) setStats(parsed.stats);
        if (parsed.solvedPuzzles) setSolvedPuzzles(parsed.solvedPuzzles);
        if (parsed.lang) setLang(parsed.lang);
        if (parsed.apiKey) setApiKey(parsed.apiKey);
      }
    } catch (e) {
      console.warn('Failed to load save', e);
    }
  }, []);

  // Save to localStorage on change
  useEffect(() => {
    localStorage.setItem(
      STORAGE_KEY,
      JSON.stringify({
        currentEra,
        unlockedEras,
        inventory,
        stats,
        solvedPuzzles,
        lang,
        apiKey
      })
    );
  }, [currentEra, unlockedEras, inventory, stats, solvedPuzzles, lang, apiKey]);

  // Handle element discovery
  const handleDiscover = (elemId) => {
    if (!inventory.includes(elemId)) {
      setInventory(prev => [...prev, elemId]);
      const el = ELEMENTS[elemId];
      if (el?.isBreakthrough) {
        setStats(prev => ({
          ...prev,
          tech: prev.tech + 15,
          stability: Math.min(100, prev.stability + 5)
        }));
      }
      checkEraProgression([...inventory, elemId], solvedPuzzles);
    }
  };

  // Handle puzzle solved
  const handleSolvePuzzle = (puzzleId) => {
    const updated = { ...solvedPuzzles, [puzzleId]: true };
    setSolvedPuzzles(updated);
    setStats(prev => ({
      ...prev,
      tech: prev.tech + 20,
      stability: Math.min(100, prev.stability + 15)
    }));
    checkEraProgression(inventory, updated);
  };

  const checkEraProgression = (currentInv, currentSolved) => {
    const eraDef = ERAS.find(e => e.id === currentEra);
    if (!eraDef) return;

    const hasAll = eraDef.requiredDiscoveries.every(req => currentInv.includes(req));
    const puzzleDone = !!currentSolved[eraDef.puzzleId];

    if (hasAll && puzzleDone && currentEra < ERAS.length) {
      const nextEra = currentEra + 1;
      if (!unlockedEras.includes(nextEra)) {
        setCurrentEra(nextEra);
        setUnlockedEras(prev => [...prev, nextEra]);
        
        // Add native raw starter elements for next era
        const newStarters = Object.values(ELEMENTS)
          .filter(e => e.era === nextEra && e.category === 'raw')
          .map(e => e.id);
        
        setInventory(prev => Array.from(new Set([...prev, ...newStarters])));
        setStats(prev => ({
          ...prev,
          food: Math.min(100, prev.food + 20),
          stability: Math.min(100, prev.stability + 20)
        }));
      }
    }
  };

  const handleSelectItem = (id) => {
    if (!slot1) setSlot1(id);
    else if (!slot2) setSlot2(id);
    else setSlot2(id);
  };

  const handleReset = () => {
    if (confirm(lang === 'hi' ? 'क्या आप सभ्यता को पुनः पत्थर युग से शुरू करना चाहते हैं?' : 'Reset all civilization progress back to Era 1?')) {
      localStorage.removeItem(STORAGE_KEY);
      setCurrentEra(1);
      setUnlockedEras([1]);
      setInventory(['stone', 'wood', 'dry_leaves']);
      setStats({ food: 40, tech: 10, stability: 50 });
      setSolvedPuzzles({});
      setSlot1(null);
      setSlot2(null);
      soundFX.playClick();
    }
  };

  const currentEraDef = ERAS.find(e => e.id === currentEra) || ERAS[0];

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen flex flex-col bg-[#0b0d14] text-slate-100">
      <Header
        era={currentEraDef}
        stats={stats}
        lang={lang}
        onToggleLang={() => setLang(prev => (prev === 'en' ? 'hi' : 'en'))}
        isMuted={isMuted}
        onToggleMute={() => setIsMuted(prev => !prev)}
        onReset={handleReset}
        onOpenSettings={() => setIsSettingsOpen(true)}
      />

      <SettlementCanvas eraId={currentEra} />

      {/* Navigation Tab Bar */}
      <nav className="flex items-center gap-2 px-6 bg-slate-900/60 border-b border-slate-800">
        <button
          onClick={() => { soundFX.playClick(); setActiveTab('forge'); }}
          className={`flex items-center gap-2 py-3 px-4 border-b-2 text-xs font-bold transition ${activeTab === 'forge' ? 'border-amber-500 text-amber-400' : 'border-transparent text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-200'}`}
        >
          <Anvil className="w-4 h-4" />
          <span>{lang === 'hi' ? 'भट्टी (प्रयोगशाला)' : 'The Forge'}</span>
        </button>

        <button
          onClick={() => { soundFX.playClick(); setActiveTab('puzzle'); }}
          className={`flex items-center gap-2 py-3 px-4 border-b-2 text-xs font-bold transition ${activeTab === 'puzzle' ? 'border-amber-500 text-amber-400' : 'border-transparent text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-200'}`}
        >
          <Puzzle className="w-4 h-4" />
          <span>{lang === 'hi' ? 'युग की पहेली' : 'Era Puzzle'}</span>
        </button>

        <button
          onClick={() => { soundFX.playClick(); setActiveTab('archive'); }}
          className={`flex items-center gap-2 py-3 px-4 border-b-2 text-xs font-bold transition ${activeTab === 'archive' ? 'border-amber-500 text-amber-400' : 'border-transparent text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-200'}`}
        >
          <BookOpen className="w-4 h-4" />
          <span>{lang === 'hi' ? 'महा अभिलेखागार' : 'Great Archive'}</span>
        </button>
      </nav>

      {/* Main Viewport */}
      <main className="flex-1 max-w-7xl w-full mx-auto p-6">
        {activeTab === 'forge' && (
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-[1fr_360px_300px] gap-6 items-start">
            <InventoryGrid
              inventory={inventory}
              onSelectItem={handleSelectItem}
              onInspectItem={(id) => setInspectId(id)}
              lang={lang}
              soundFX={soundFX}
            />

            <Crucible
              slot1={slot1}
              slot2={slot2}
              onClearSlot={(slotNum) => (slotNum === 1 ? setSlot1(null) : setSlot2(null))}
              onClearAll={() => { setSlot1(null); setSlot2(null); }}
              onSelectResult={(id) => setInspectId(id)}
              onDiscover={handleDiscover}
              lang={lang}
              soundFX={soundFX}
            />

            <div className="flex flex-col gap-5">
              <ElementInspector selectedElementId={inspectId} lang={lang} />
              <MentorDrawer eraId={currentEra} lang={lang} apiKey={apiKey} soundFX={soundFX} />
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {activeTab === 'puzzle' && (
          <div>
            <LeveragePuzzle
              onSolve={handleSolvePuzzle}
              isSolved={!!solvedPuzzles['leverage_puzzle']}
              lang={lang}
              soundFX={soundFX}
            />
          </div>
        )}

        {activeTab === 'archive' && (
          <GreatArchive
            currentEra={currentEra}
            unlockedEras={unlockedEras}
            inventory={inventory}
            lang={lang}
          />
        )}
      </main>

      {/* Settings Modal */}
      {isSettingsOpen && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/80 backdrop-blur-sm z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4">
          <div className="bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 rounded-2xl p-6 max-w-md w-full shadow-2xl">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-base font-bold text-slate-100 mb-2">
              <KeyRound className="w-5 h-5 text-amber-400" />
              <h3>Mentor Settings</h3>
            </div>
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 leading-relaxed mb-4">
              <b>Offline Mode:</b> Active by default with Socratic hints for all 8 eras.<br/>
              <b>Live AI Mode (Optional):</b> Enter your Google Gemini API key to enable live conversational reasoning.
            </p>
            <input
              type="password"
              value={apiKey}
              onChange={(e) => setApiKey(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="Enter Gemini API key..."
              className="w-full bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 rounded-xl px-3 py-2 text-xs text-slate-100 focus:outline-none focus:border-amber-500 mb-5"
            />
            <div className="flex justify-end gap-2">
              <button
                onClick={() => setIsSettingsOpen(false)}
                className="px-4 py-2 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-300 transition"
              >
                Close
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}



--- FILE: src\components\Crucible.jsx ---
import React from 'react';
import { Sparkles, X, Plus } from 'lucide-react';
import confetti from 'canvas-confetti';
import { ELEMENTS, RECIPES } from '../data/gameData';

export function Crucible({ slot1, slot2, onClearSlot, onClearAll, onSelectResult, onDiscover, lang, soundFX }) {
  const [resultMsg, setResultMsg] = React.useState(null);

  const handleSynthesize = () => {
    if (!slot1 || !slot2) {
      soundFX.playError();
      setResultMsg({
        success: false,
        text: lang === 'hi' ? 'भट्टी में दो तत्व रखें!' : 'Place two elements in the Crucible to experiment!'
      });
      return;
    }

    const recipe = RECIPES.find(
      r => (r.inputs[0] === slot1 && r.inputs[1] === slot2) || (r.inputs[0] === slot2 && r.inputs[1] === slot1)
    );

    if (recipe) {
      soundFX.playCraft();
      confetti({
        particleCount: 50,
        spread: 60,
        origin: { y: 0.7 }
      });
      setTimeout(() => soundFX.playDiscover(), 200);

      const resElement = ELEMENTS[recipe.result];
      onDiscover(recipe.result);
      onSelectResult(recipe.result);

      setResultMsg({
        success: true,
        text: `${lang === 'hi' ? '🎉 नया आविष्कार:' : '🎉 EUREKA! Synthesized:'} ${resElement.name[lang]}`,
        story: recipe.story[lang]
      });

      onClearAll();
    } else {
      soundFX.playError();
      const name1 = ELEMENTS[slot1]?.name[lang] || slot1;
      const name2 = ELEMENTS[slot2]?.name[lang] || slot2;
      setResultMsg({
        success: false,
        text: lang === 'hi' 
          ? `${name1} और ${name2} के बीच कोई रासायनिक क्रिया नहीं हुई। इनके गुणों पर विचार करें!`
          : `No reaction between ${name1} and ${name2}. Think about their physical properties!`
      });
    }
  };

  const elem1 = slot1 ? ELEMENTS[slot1] : null;
  const elem2 = slot2 ? ELEMENTS[slot2] : null;

  return (
    <div className="bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 rounded-2xl p-6 flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-center shadow-xl">
      <h2 className="text-lg font-bold flex items-center gap-2 text-slate-100">
        <Sparkles className="w-5 h-5 text-amber-400" />
        {lang === 'hi' ? 'वैज्ञानिक भट्टी (क्रूसिबल)' : 'The Crucible'}
      </h2>
      <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 mt-1 mb-6">
        {lang === 'hi' ? 'दो तत्वों को मिलाकर नए वैज्ञानिक अनुसंधानों को जन्म दें।' : 'Combine elements to forge civilization breakthroughs.'}
      </p>

      {/* Crucible Slots */}
      <div className="flex items-center gap-4 my-2">
        {/* Slot 1 */}
        <div className={`w-24 h-24 rounded-2xl border-2 flex flex-col items-center justify-center relative transition ${elem1 ? 'border-amber-500 bg-slate-800/80 shadow-lg shadow-amber-500/10' : 'border-dashed border-slate-700 bg-slate-950/60'}`}>
          {elem1 ? (
            <>
              <span className="text-3xl">{elem1.icon}</span>
              <span className="text-[11px] font-semibold text-slate-200 mt-1 line-clamp-1 px-1">{elem1.name[lang]}</span>
              <button
                onClick={() => onClearSlot(1)}
                className="absolute -top-2 -right-2 p-1 rounded-full bg-rose-500 text-white hover:bg-rose-600 transition"
              >
                <X className="w-3 h-3" />
              </button>
            </>
          ) : (
            <span className="text-xs text-slate-500">{lang === 'hi' ? '+ तत्व 1' : '+ Item 1'}</span>
          )}
        </div>

        <Plus className="w-6 h-6 text-slate-600" />

        {/* Slot 2 */}
        <div className={`w-24 h-24 rounded-2xl border-2 flex flex-col items-center justify-center relative transition ${elem2 ? 'border-amber-500 bg-slate-800/80 shadow-lg shadow-amber-500/10' : 'border-dashed border-slate-700 bg-slate-950/60'}`}>
          {elem2 ? (
            <>
              <span className="text-3xl">{elem2.icon}</span>
              <span className="text-[11px] font-semibold text-slate-200 mt-1 line-clamp-1 px-1">{elem2.name[lang]}</span>
              <button
                onClick={() => onClearSlot(2)}
                className="absolute -top-2 -right-2 p-1 rounded-full bg-rose-500 text-white hover:bg-rose-600 transition"
              >
                <X className="w-3 h-3" />
              </button>
            </>
          ) : (
            <span className="text-xs text-slate-500">{lang === 'hi' ? '+ तत्व 2' : '+ Item 2'}</span>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      <button
        onClick={handleSynthesize}
        className="mt-6 w-full max-w-xs py-3 px-6 rounded-xl font-bold text-sm bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-500 via-orange-500 to-amber-600 text-white shadow-lg shadow-orange-500/25 hover:shadow-orange-500/40 hover:-translate-y-0.5 transition active:translate-y-0"
      >
        {lang === 'hi' ? 'संश्लेषण करें (Forge)' : 'Forge / Synthesize'}
      </button>

      {(slot1 || slot2) && (
        <button onClick={onClearAll} className="mt-2 text-xs text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-200 underline">
          {lang === 'hi' ? 'भट्टी खाली करें' : 'Clear Crucible'}
        </button>
      )}

      {/* Result notification */}
      {resultMsg && (
        <div className={`mt-4 p-3 rounded-xl text-xs w-full text-left border ${resultMsg.success ? 'bg-emerald-500/10 border-emerald-500/30 text-emerald-300' : 'bg-rose-500/10 border-rose-500/30 text-rose-300'}`}>
          <div className="font-semibold">{resultMsg.text}</div>
          {resultMsg.story && <div className="mt-1 text-slate-300">{resultMsg.story}</div>}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

export function ElementInspector({ selectedElementId, lang }) {

      </span>

        {el.desc[lang]}
      </p>

          </div>

            {el.science[lang]}
          </p>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}



--- FILE: src\components\GreatArchive.jsx ---
import React from 'react';
import { ERAS, ELEMENTS } from '../data/gameData';
import { CheckCircle2, Lock } from 'lucide-react';

export function GreatArchive({ currentEra, unlockedEras, inventory, lang }) {
  return (
    <div className="max-w-4xl mx-auto flex flex-col gap-5 py-4">
      {ERAS.map(era => {
        const isUnlocked = unlockedEras.includes(era.id);
        const isCurrent = era.id === currentEra;
        const isPast = unlockedEras.includes(era.id) && era.id < currentEra;

        return (
          <div
            key={era.id}
            className={`p-6 rounded-2xl border transition ${isCurrent ? 'bg-slate-900 border-amber-500 shadow-xl shadow-amber-500/10' : isUnlocked ? 'bg-slate-900/80 border-slate-800' : 'bg-slate-950/40 border-slate-900 opacity-40'}`}
          >
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-3">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                <span className="text-3xl">{era.badge}</span>
                <div>
                  <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-slate-100">
                    Era {era.id}: {era.name[lang]}
                  </h3>
                  <span className="text-xs text-slate-400">{era.period[lang]}</span>
                </div>
              </div>
              <span className={`text-xs px-3 py-1 rounded-full font-bold uppercase tracking-wider ${isCurrent ? 'bg-amber-500/20 text-amber-400 border border-amber-500/40' : isPast ? 'bg-emerald-500/20 text-emerald-400 border border-emerald-500/30' : 'bg-slate-800 text-slate-500'}`}>
                {isCurrent ? 'Active Era' : isPast ? 'Completed' : 'Locked'}
              </span>
            </div>

            <blockquote className="border-l-2 border-amber-500 pl-3 italic text-xs text-amber-300/90 my-2">
              "{era.quote[lang]}"
            </blockquote>

            <p className="text-xs text-slate-300 mt-2 leading-relaxed">
              {era.story[lang]}
            </p>

            <div className="mt-3 p-3 rounded-xl bg-slate-950/70 border border-slate-800 text-xs text-slate-300">
              <b className="text-cyan-400">⚡ Scientific Principle: </b>
              {era.scienceFact[lang]}
            </div>

            <div className="mt-4 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
              <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-400">Required:</span>
              {era.requiredDiscoveries.map(reqId => {
                const has = inventory.includes(reqId);
                const name = ELEMENTS[reqId]?.name[lang] || reqId;
                return (
                  <span
                    key={reqId}
                    className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg text-xs font-medium border ${has ? 'bg-emerald-500/10 border-emerald-500/30 text-emerald-300' : 'bg-slate-800/40 border-slate-800 text-slate-500'}`}
                  >
                    {has ? <CheckCircle2 className="w-3 h-3 text-emerald-400" /> : <Lock className="w-3 h-3 text-slate-500" />}
                    {name}
                  </span>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          </div>
        );
      })}
    </div>
  );
}

--- FILE: src\components\InventoryGrid.jsx ---
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { Package, Sparkles } from 'lucide-react';
import { ELEMENTS } from '../data/gameData';

export function InventoryGrid({ inventory, onSelectItem, onInspectItem, lang, soundFX }) {
  const [filterEra, setFilterEra] = useState('all');

  const items = inventory.map(id => ELEMENTS[id]).filter(Boolean);
  const filteredItems = filterEra === 'all' ? items : items.filter(el => el.era === parseInt(filterEra, 10));

  return (
    <div className="bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 rounded-2xl flex flex-col h-[560px] overflow-hidden shadow-xl">
      <div className="px-5 py-4 border-b border-slate-800 flex items-center justify-between">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 font-bold text-sm text-slate-100">
          <Package className="w-4 h-4 text-cyan-400" />
          <span>{lang === 'hi' ? 'खोजे गए तत्व' : 'Discovered Elements'}</span>
        </div>
        <span className="text-xs bg-slate-800 text-slate-300 px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full font-semibold">
          {inventory.length} / {Object.keys(ELEMENTS).length}
        </span>
      </div>

      {/* Filter Tabs */}
      <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-4 py-2 border-b border-slate-800/60 overflow-x-auto text-xs">
        <button
          onClick={() => setFilterEra('all')}
          className={`px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg transition ${filterEra === 'all' ? 'bg-amber-500/20 text-amber-400 font-bold border border-amber-500/40' : 'text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-200'}`}
        >
          All
        </button>
        {[1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7, 8].map(eraNum => (
          <button
            key={eraNum}
            onClick={() => setFilterEra(eraNum.toString())}
            className={`px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg transition whitespace-nowrap ${filterEra === eraNum.toString() ? 'bg-amber-500/20 text-amber-400 font-bold border border-amber-500/40' : 'text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-200'}`}
          >
            Era {eraNum}
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>

      {/* Grid of items */}
      <div className="flex-1 p-4 overflow-y-auto grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-3 content-start">
        {filteredItems.map(el => (
          <div
            key={el.id}
            onClick={() => {
              soundFX.playClick();
              onSelectItem(el.id);
              onInspectItem(el.id);
            }}
            className={`group relative p-3 rounded-xl border flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-center cursor-pointer transition select-none hover:-translate-y-1 hover:shadow-lg ${el.isBreakthrough ? 'bg-slate-800/80 border-amber-500/40 hover:border-amber-400 hover:shadow-amber-500/20' : 'bg-slate-950/70 border-slate-800 hover:border-slate-700'}`}
          >
            {el.isBreakthrough && (
              <span className="absolute top-1.5 right-1.5 text-amber-400">
                <Sparkles className="w-3 h-3" />
              </span>
            )}
            <span className="text-3xl mb-1 group-hover:scale-110 transition">{el.icon}</span>
            <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-200 line-clamp-1">{el.name[lang]}</span>
            <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-500 mt-0.5">Era {el.era}</span>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}



--- FILE: src\components\MentorDrawer.jsx ---
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { Bot, Lightbulb, Send } from 'lucide-react';

const OFFLINE_HINTS = {
  1: {
    en: "Fire requires three pillars: fuel, oxygen, and ignition temperature. Twirling dry wood vigorously turns kinetic energy into friction heat!",
    hi: "आग के लिए ईंधन, ऑक्सीजन और गर्मी चाहिए। सूखी लकड़ियों को तेज़ी से रगड़ने से गतिज ऊर्जा घर्षण ताप में बदलती है!"
  },
  2: {
    en: "Baking molded clay inside your kiln vitrifies the minerals into waterproof ceramic pottery!",
    hi: "मिट्टी को आग में पकाने से सिलिकेट सख्त होकर जलरोधी बर्तन बनाते हैं।"
  },
  3: {
    en: "Pure copper bends under stress. Fusing it with molten tin creates bronze, which is ten billion percent tougher!",
    hi: "तांबे में टिन मिलाने से धातु के परमाणुओं का फिसलना रुक जाता है और मजबूत कांसा बनता है!"
  }
};

export function MentorDrawer({ eraId, lang, apiKey, soundFX }) {
  const [messages, setMessages] = useState([
    {
      role: 'mentor',
      text: lang === 'hi'
        ? "पत्थर की दुनिया में स्वागत है! हमारा दिमाग ही हमारा सबसे बड़ा हथियार है। पत्थरों को टकराकर पहला धारदार औज़ार बनाएं!"
        : "Welcome to the Stone World! Our scientific mind is our greatest weapon. Strike stones together to knap your first cutting blade!"
    }
  ]);
  const [inputVal, setInputVal] = useState('');
  const [isTyping, setIsTyping] = useState(false);

  const handleSend = async (queryText) => {
    const q = (queryText || inputVal).trim();
    if (!q) return;

    soundFX.playClick();
    setMessages(prev => [...prev, { role: 'user', text: q }]);
    setInputVal('');
    setIsTyping(true);

    // If live API key is set
    if (apiKey && apiKey.length > 10) {
      try {
        const url = `https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/models/gemini-1.5-flash:generateContent?key=${apiKey}`;
        const res = await fetch(url, {
          method: 'POST',
          headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
          body: JSON.stringify({
            contents: [{ role: 'user', parts: [{ text: `You are the Science Mentor in the game The Stone World (inspired by Dr. STONE's Senku). Answer enthusiastically and Socratically in ${lang === 'hi' ? 'Hindi' : 'English'}.\nQuestion: ${q}` }] }]
          })
        });
        const data = await res.json();
        const reply = data.candidates?.[0]?.content?.parts?.[0]?.text;
        if (reply) {
          setMessages(prev => [...prev, { role: 'mentor', text: reply }]);
          setIsTyping(false);
          soundFX.playClick();
          return;
        }
      } catch (e) {
        console.warn('API error, using offline hint', e);
      }
    }

    // Offline heuristic response
    setTimeout(() => {
      const hint = OFFLINE_HINTS[eraId]?.[lang] || (lang === 'hi' ? "वैज्ञानिक सोच ही सभ्यता को आगे बढ़ाएगी!" : "Scientific curiosity will carry civilization forward!");
      setMessages(prev => [...prev, { role: 'mentor', text: hint }]);
      setIsTyping(false);
      soundFX.playClick();
    }, 400);
  };

  return (
    <div className="bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 rounded-2xl flex flex-col flex-1 shadow-xl overflow-hidden min-h-[220px]">
      <div className="px-4 py-3 border-b border-slate-800 flex items-center justify-between">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 font-bold text-xs text-slate-100">
          <Bot className="w-4 h-4 text-cyan-400" />
          <span>{lang === 'hi' ? 'वैज्ञानिक मार्गदर्शक (AI Mentor)' : 'Science Mentor'}</span>
        </div>
        <button
          onClick={() => handleSend(lang === 'hi' ? 'मुझे एक वैज्ञानिक संकेत दें' : 'Give me a scientific clue')}
          className="flex items-center gap-1 text-[11px] font-semibold text-amber-400 bg-amber-500/10 hover:bg-amber-500/20 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg border border-amber-500/30 transition"
        >
          <Lightbulb className="w-3 h-3" />
          <span>Hint</span>
        </button>
      </div>

      <div className="flex-1 p-3 overflow-y-auto flex flex-col gap-2 text-xs max-h-48">
        {messages.map((m, idx) => (
          <div
            key={idx}
            className={`p-2.5 rounded-xl max-w-[88%] leading-relaxed ${m.role === 'user' ? 'bg-slate-800 text-slate-100 self-end' : 'bg-cyan-950/40 border-l-2 border-cyan-400 text-cyan-200 self-start'}`}
          >
            {m.text}
          </div>
        ))}
        {isTyping && <div className="text-xs text-slate-500 italic">Mentor is thinking...</div>}
      </div>

      <div className="p-2 border-t border-slate-800 flex items-center gap-1.5">
        <input
          type="text"
          value={inputVal}
          onChange={(e) => setInputVal(e.target.value)}
          onKeyDown={(e) => e.key === 'Enter' && handleSend()}
          placeholder={lang === 'hi' ? 'विज्ञान से जुड़ा प्रश्न पूछें...' : 'Ask mentor a science question...'}
          className="flex-1 bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 rounded-lg px-3 py-1.5 text-xs text-slate-100 focus:outline-none focus:border-cyan-500"
        />
        <button
          onClick={() => handleSend()}
          className="p-1.5 rounded-lg bg-cyan-600 hover:bg-cyan-500 text-white transition"
        >
          <Send className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}



--- FILE: src\components\SettlementCanvas.jsx ---
import React, { useEffect, useRef } from 'react';

export function SettlementCanvas({ eraId }) {
  const canvasRef = useRef(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    const canvas = canvasRef.current;
    if (!canvas) return;
    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
    const w = canvas.width;
    const h = canvas.height;

    ctx.clearRect(0, 0, w, h);

    // Sky gradient
    const sky = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, 0, h);
    if (eraId <= 2) {
      sky.addColorStop(0, '#10121d');
      sky.addColorStop(1, '#2c1810');
    } else if (eraId <= 4) {
      sky.addColorStop(0, '#1a2332');
      sky.addColorStop(1, '#78281f');
    } else if (eraId <= 6) {
      sky.addColorStop(0, '#1e272e');
      sky.addColorStop(1, '#485460');
    } else {
      sky.addColorStop(0, '#0a3d62');
      sky.addColorStop(1, '#00cec9');
    }
    ctx.fillStyle = sky;
    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, w, h);

    // Stars
    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.7)';
    for (let i = 0; i < 28; i++) {
      const sx = (i * 47) % w;
      const sy = (i * 23) % (h * 0.45);
      ctx.fillRect(sx, sy, 1.5, 1.5);
    }

    // Terrain silhouette
    ctx.fillStyle = eraId >= 4 ? '#1e272e' : '#3d2314';
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(0, h);
    ctx.lineTo(0, h - 30);
    ctx.quadraticCurveTo(w * 0.3, h - 45, w * 0.6, h - 25);
    ctx.quadraticCurveTo(w * 0.85, h - 15, w, h - 35);
    ctx.lineTo(w, h);
    ctx.fill();

    // Foreground structures according to era
    if (eraId === 1) {
      // Cave
      ctx.fillStyle = '#111419';
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(80, h - 25, 45, Math.PI, 0);
      ctx.fill();

      // Roaring Campfire
      ctx.fillStyle = '#e67e22';
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(180, h - 28, 12, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.fill();
      ctx.fillStyle = '#f1c40f';
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(180, h - 30, 7, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.fill();
    } else if (eraId === 2) {
      // Thatched hut
      ctx.fillStyle = '#6d4c41';
      ctx.fillRect(110, h - 45, 40, 20);
      ctx.fillStyle = '#bcaaa4';
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.moveTo(105, h - 45);
      ctx.lineTo(130, h - 65);
      ctx.lineTo(155, h - 45);
      ctx.fill();

      // Crops
      ctx.strokeStyle = '#2ecc71';
      ctx.lineWidth = 2;
      for (let x = 180; x < 320; x += 10) {
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(x, h - 25);
        ctx.lineTo(x, h - 40);
        ctx.stroke();
      }
    } else if (eraId <= 4) {
      // Brick workshops and classical columns
      ctx.fillStyle = '#c0392b';
      ctx.fillRect(120, h - 55, 55, 30);
      ctx.fillStyle = '#ecf0f1';
      ctx.fillRect(240, h - 65, 8, 40);
      ctx.fillRect(260, h - 65, 8, 40);
      ctx.fillRect(280, h - 65, 8, 40);
      ctx.fillRect(235, h - 70, 58, 6);
    } else if (eraId <= 6) {
      // Smokestacks and steam puffs
      ctx.fillStyle = '#2c3e50';
      ctx.fillRect(140, h - 75, 18, 50);
      ctx.fillRect(180, h - 95, 22, 70);
      ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(236, 240, 241, 0.4)';
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(149, h - 90, 14, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.arc(191, h - 110, 20, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.fill();
    } else {
      // Silicon skyline & solar arrays
      ctx.fillStyle = '#00cec9';
      ctx.fillRect(140, h - 95, 30, 70);
      ctx.fillRect(190, h - 115, 40, 90);
      ctx.fillRect(250, h - 85, 30, 60);
      ctx.fillStyle = '#ffeaa7';
      for (let y = h - 105; y < h - 35; y += 14) {
        ctx.fillRect(200, y, 6, 6);
        ctx.fillRect(214, y, 6, 6);
      }
    }
  }, [eraId]);

  return (
    <div className="w-full h-24 bg-black border-b border-slate-800 relative overflow-hidden">
      <canvas ref={canvasRef} width={1400} height={96} className="w-full h-full block" />
    </div>
  );
}

--- FILE: src\hooks\useSoundFX.js ---
// Web Audio Hook
import { useRef } from 'react';

export function useSoundFX(isMuted) {
  const ctxRef = useRef(null);

  const getCtx = () => {
    if (!ctxRef.current) {
      const AudioCtx = window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext;
      if (AudioCtx) ctxRef.current = new AudioCtx();
    }
    if (ctxRef.current?.state === 'suspended') {
      ctxRef.current.resume();
    }
    return ctxRef.current;
  };

  const playClick = () => {
    if (isMuted) return;
    const ctx = getCtx();
    if (!ctx) return;
    const osc = ctx.createOscillator();
    const gain = ctx.createGain();
    osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(600, ctx.currentTime);
    osc.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(200, ctx.currentTime + 0.05);
    gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.1, ctx.currentTime);
    gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, ctx.currentTime + 0.05);
    osc.connect(gain);
    gain.connect(ctx.destination);
    osc.start();
    osc.stop(ctx.currentTime + 0.05);
  };

  const playCraft = () => {
    if (isMuted) return;
    const ctx = getCtx();
    if (!ctx) return;
    const osc = ctx.createOscillator();
    const gain = ctx.createGain();
    osc.type = 'triangle';
    osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(220, ctx.currentTime);
    osc.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(880, ctx.currentTime + 0.25);
    gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.15, ctx.currentTime);
    gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.01, ctx.currentTime + 0.28);
    osc.connect(gain);
    gain.connect(ctx.destination);
    osc.start();
    osc.stop(ctx.currentTime + 0.28);
  };

  const playDiscover = () => {
    if (isMuted) return;
    const ctx = getCtx();
    if (!ctx) return;
    [523.25, 659.25, 783.99, 1046.50].forEach((freq, i) => {
      const osc = ctx.createOscillator();
      const gain = ctx.createGain();
      const t = ctx.currentTime + i * 0.08;
      osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(freq, t);
      gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.15, t);
      gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, t + 0.3);
      osc.connect(gain);
      gain.connect(ctx.destination);
      osc.start(t);
      osc.stop(t + 0.3);
    });
  };

  const playError = () => {
    if (isMuted) return;
    const ctx = getCtx();
    if (!ctx) return;
    const osc = ctx.createOscillator();
    const gain = ctx.createGain();
    osc.type = 'sawtooth';
    osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(140, ctx.currentTime);
    osc.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(60, ctx.currentTime + 0.15);
    gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.12, ctx.currentTime);
    gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, ctx.currentTime + 0.15);
    osc.connect(gain);
    gain.connect(ctx.destination);
    osc.start();
    osc.stop(ctx.currentTime + 0.15);
  };

  return { playClick, playCraft, playDiscover, playError };
}

--- FILE: src\puzzles\LeveragePuzzle.jsx ---
import React, { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import { Scale, CheckCircle2 } from 'lucide-react';
import confetti from 'canvas-confetti';

export function LeveragePuzzle({ onSolve, isSolved, lang, soundFX }) {
  const [fulcrumRatio, setFulcrumRatio] = useState(0.3); // 30% from left
  const [lifted, setLifted] = useState(isSolved);
  const canvasRef = useRef(null);

  const boulderWeight = 500;
  const hunterWeight = 70;

  const leftArm = fulcrumRatio * 6;
  const rightArm = (1 - fulcrumRatio) * 6;
  const loadTorque = Math.round(boulderWeight * 9.8 * leftArm);
  const effortTorque = Math.round(hunterWeight * 9.8 * rightArm);
  const canLift = effortTorque >= loadTorque;

  useEffect(() => {
    const canvas = canvasRef.current;
    if (!canvas) return;
    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
    const w = canvas.width;
    const h = canvas.height;
    const groundY = 180;
    const beamStartX = 70;
    const beamLength = 480;

    ctx.clearRect(0, 0, w, h);

    // Ground
    ctx.fillStyle = '#1e293b';
    ctx.fillRect(0, groundY, w, 60);

    const fulcrumX = beamStartX + beamLength * fulcrumRatio;
    const fulcrumY = groundY;

    // Fulcrum triangle
    ctx.fillStyle = '#f59e0b';
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(fulcrumX, fulcrumY - 35);
    ctx.lineTo(fulcrumX - 22, fulcrumY);
    ctx.lineTo(fulcrumX + 22, fulcrumY);
    ctx.closePath();
    ctx.fill();

    // Beam angle
    const angle = lifted ? -0.12 : (fulcrumRatio < 0.22 ? 0.05 : 0);

    ctx.save();
    ctx.translate(fulcrumX, fulcrumY - 35);
    ctx.rotate(angle);

    const leftLen = fulcrumX - beamStartX;
    const rightLen = beamLength - leftLen;

    // Beam
    ctx.fillStyle = '#b45309';
    ctx.fillRect(-leftLen, -8, beamLength, 16);

    // Megalith boulder on left
    const boulderY = -8 - (lifted ? 52 : 36);
    ctx.fillStyle = '#64748b';
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(-leftLen + 20, boulderY, 32, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    ctx.fill();
    ctx.fillStyle = '#fff';
    ctx.font = 'bold 11px sans-serif';
    ctx.fillText('500 kg', -leftLen + 3, boulderY + 4);

    // Hunter pushing on right
    ctx.fillStyle = '#ef4444';
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(rightLen - 20, -32, 12, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    ctx.fill();
    ctx.strokeStyle = '#dc2626';
    ctx.lineWidth = 3;
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(rightLen - 20, -20);
    ctx.lineTo(rightLen - 20, -4);
    ctx.stroke();
    ctx.fillStyle = '#fff';
    ctx.fillText('70 kg', rightLen - 34, -42);

    ctx.restore();
  }, [fulcrumRatio, lifted]);

  const handleApplyForce = () => {
    if (canLift) {
      setLifted(true);
      soundFX.playDiscover();
      confetti({ particleCount: 60, spread: 70 });
      onSolve('leverage_puzzle');
    } else {
      soundFX.playError();
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="max-w-2xl mx-auto bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 rounded-2xl p-6 shadow-xl">
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-base font-bold text-amber-400 mb-1">
        <Scale className="w-5 h-5" />
        <h3>{lang === 'hi' ? 'उत्तोलक और आधार (लीवर पहेली)' : 'The Great Megalith: Leverage & Fulcrum'}</h3>
      </div>
      <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 mb-5 leading-relaxed">
        {lang === 'hi'
          ? 'गुफा के मुख पर 500 किग्रा का विशाल पत्थर गिरा है। शिकारी का भार केवल 70 किग्रा है। आधार को पत्थर के करीब खिसकाएं ताकि टॉर्क पत्थर को उठा सके!'
          : 'A massive 500 kg megalith blocks the cave entrance. The hunter weighs only 70 kg. Slide the fulcrum closer to the boulder to multiply torque!'}
      </p>

      <div className="bg-slate-950 rounded-xl overflow-hidden border border-slate-800 mb-5">
        <canvas ref={canvasRef} width={620} height={220} className="w-full h-auto block" />
      </div>

      <div className="flex flex-col gap-3">
        <div className="flex justify-between text-xs text-slate-300 font-medium">
          <span>Fulcrum Position: <b>{Math.round(fulcrumRatio * 100)}%</b></span>
          <span>Effort Torque: <b className="text-amber-400">{effortTorque} Nm</b></span>
          <span>Load Torque: <b className="text-slate-400">{loadTorque} Nm</b></span>
        </div>

        <input
          type="range"
          min="10"
          max="90"
          value={Math.round(fulcrumRatio * 100)}
          disabled={isSolved}
          onChange={(e) => {
            setFulcrumRatio(e.target.value / 100);
            setLifted(false);
          }}
          className="w-full accent-amber-500 cursor-pointer"
        />

        <div className="flex items-center justify-between mt-3">
          <div className={`text-xs font-bold ${canLift ? 'text-emerald-400' : 'text-rose-400'}`}>
            {canLift 
              ? (lang === 'hi' ? '✓ टॉर्क पर्याप्त है! पत्थर उठाया जा सकता है!' : '✓ Force exceeds boulder resistance!')
              : (lang === 'hi' ? '✗ बल अपर्याप्त है। आधार को पत्थर के करीब लाएं!' : '✗ Insufficient torque. Move fulcrum closer to rock!')}
          </div>

          <button
            onClick={handleApplyForce}
            disabled={isSolved}
            className={`px-5 py-2 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold transition flex items-center gap-1.5 ${isSolved ? 'bg-emerald-600 text-white cursor-default' : 'bg-gradient-to-r from-emerald-500 to-teal-600 text-white hover:shadow-lg hover:shadow-teal-500/20 active:scale-95'}`}
          >
            {isSolved ? (
              <>
                <CheckCircle2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                <span>{lang === 'hi' ? 'सफल (हल हो चुका)' : 'Hoisted & Solved'}</span>
              </>
            ) : (
              <span>{lang === 'hi' ? 'ताकत लगाएं!' : 'Apply Force!'}</span>
            )}
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
